# Third-party notices

featherplay is MIT-licensed (see [LICENSE](LICENSE)). It is built from the Rust crates
listed below, embeds fonts and a JavaScript engine, and downloads two programs on first run.
This file covers its four builds: `featherplay.exe` for Windows x64 and ARM64, and
`featherplay` for Linux x86_64 and aarch64 (the AppImage and the `.tar.gz` hold the same
binary). The crate list is generated by
[cargo-about](https://github.com/EmbarkStudios/cargo-about), once for each build's target
(build and dev dependencies left out), and names the builds a crate is in when it isn't in
all four. Regenerate it with:

```
python3 packaging/third-party/generate.py
```

## Downloaded on first run (not bundled)

| Program | Licence | Source |
|---|---|---|
| yt-dlp | The Unlicense | https://github.com/yt-dlp/yt-dlp |
| Deno | MIT License | https://github.com/denoland/deno |

featherplay downloads both from their GitHub releases into `%LOCALAPPDATA%\featherplay\sidecars`
on Windows and `~/.cache/featherplay/sidecars` on Linux (yt-dlp 2026.10.26 and Deno 2.9.8)
and checks each file against a SHA256 hash pinned in featherplay's code
(`crates/streams/src/sidecars.rs`); a file that doesn't match is deleted.
When GitHub can't be reached, Deno comes from Deno's own download server, dl.deno.land, whose
zip has its own pinned hash, and on Windows the same yt-dlp comes from featherplay's site
(unchanged, under the same hash).
After that, yt-dlp updates itself with its own updater (`yt-dlp -U`) at most once a day. They
are separate programs that featherplay runs; they are not linked into it.

## Embedded scripts

featherplay embeds yt-dlp's challenge solver scripts (yt-dlp-ejs 0.8.0,
https://github.com/yt-dlp/ejs) in `crates/streams/ejs/` and runs them in QuickJS-NG (below)
to prepare each new YouTube player once and to answer each song's challenges. `yt.solver.core.min.js` is under The Unlicense.
`yt.solver.lib.min.js` bundles two libraries, whose notices are kept at the top of the file:

| Library | Licence | Source |
|---|---|---|
| meriyah 6.1.4 | ISC License, Copyright (c) 2019 and later, KFlash and others | https://github.com/meriyah/meriyah |
| astring 1.9.0 | MIT License, Copyright (c) 2015, David Bonnet | https://github.com/davidbonnet/astring |

## Embedded JavaScript engine

| Engine | Licence | Source |
|---|---|---|
| QuickJS-NG 0.16.2 | MIT License, Copyright (c) 2017-2026 Fabrice Bellard, (c) 2017-2024 Charlie Gordon, (c) 2023-2026 Ben Noordhuis, (c) 2023-2026 Saúl Ibarra Corretgé | https://github.com/quickjs-ng/quickjs |

QuickJS-NG is C, compiled into the binary by the `rquickjs-sys` crate (listed below
with `rquickjs` and `rquickjs-core`, MIT), whose package carries its `quickjs/LICENSE`.

## Embedded fonts

| Font | Licence | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Manrope | SIL Open Font License 1.1 | https://github.com/googlefonts/manrope |
| Inter 4.1, a Latin subset (Linux build only) | SIL Open Font License 1.1 | https://github.com/rsms/inter |

Manrope's full licence is in `apps/featherplay/ui/fonts/manrope/OFL.txt` and on the About page;
Inter's is in `apps/featherplay/ui/fonts/inter/OFL.txt`, in the font's own name table, and on
the Linux build's About page. Both texts are also under [Font licences](#font-licences) below.

## Slint

The Slint crates (`slint`, `i-slint-*`) are triple-licensed: GPL-3.0-only, the Slint
Royalty-free Desktop, Mobile, and Web Applications License 2.0, or the Slint Software
License 3.0. featherplay uses them under the **Royalty-free License 2.0**
(https://github.com/slint-ui/slint/blob/master/LICENSES/LicenseRef-Slint-Royalty-free-2.0.md),
which is why the About page shows the "Made with Slint" badge. `i-slint-renderer-skia` is
built from a modified copy in `vendor/i-slint-renderer-skia/` that leaves out its wgpu
support; that licence allows modified copies, and the copy keeps its `LICENSES/`.

## Skia

The window is drawn by Skia, a C++ library linked into the binary through the `skia-bindings`
crate, from rust-skia's prebuilt binaries: skia-bindings 0.153.3, Skia commit
`61e7ca4e99062cdd0ab69445d5963fb3365778f6` (github.com/rust-skia/skia), built with the
features `d3d`, `ganesh`, `gl`, `jpeg` and `pdf` for Windows, and `ganesh`, `gl`, `jpeg`,
`pdf` and `vulkan` for Linux. The crate itself is MIT; Skia is under the BSD 3-Clause License:


### Libraries inside Skia

Skia's build compiles these libraries into the same binary. The versions are the ones Skia's
`DEPS` file pins at that commit.

| Library | Version | Licence | Used for |
|---|---|---|---|
| zlib (Chromium's copy) | 1.3.0.1 | Zlib | compression in PNG and PDF |
| libpng | 1.6.56 | PNG Reference Library License version 2 | PNG images |
| libjpeg-turbo | 3.1.0 | IJG License, BSD 3-Clause, Zlib | JPEG images |
| Wuffs | 0.3.3 | Apache License 2.0 | GIF images |
| Expat | 2.7.4 | MIT License | XML |

This software is based in part on the work of the Independent JPEG Group.

HarfBuzz, ICU and FreeType are not in featherplay.exe: the prebuilt Skia includes them only
with the `textlayout` feature, or on platforms other than Windows. D3D12MemoryAllocator and
SPIRV-Cross are in the prebuilt library, but nothing featherplay calls reaches them, so the
linker leaves them out. (Checked on 2026-11-01 against the object files in the prebuilt
`skia.lib` and the strings in a release `featherplay.exe`. Check again when skia-bindings
changes version or features.)

On Linux, Skia draws text with the system's FreeType and Fontconfig, shared libraries that
every Linux desktop has; featherplay links to them and doesn't ship them. HarfBuzz and ICU
are left out there too (no `textlayout`). The Linux binary has none of the libraries in the
table above, nor the Vulkan Memory Allocator that the `vulkan` feature puts in the prebuilt
library: nothing featherplay calls reaches them. (Checked on 2026-11-12 against the object
files in the prebuilt `libskia.a` and the symbols of a Linux x86_64 build of featherplay.)
Their notices stay here, as the Windows builds need them.

## Opus decoder

Opus streams are decoded by the `opus-decoder` crate (MIT OR Apache-2.0), a Rust port of
libopus, built from a modified copy in `vendor/opus-decoder/` (a faster FFT and a 32-bit
collapse mask; see its `VENDORED.md`). libopus is under the BSD 3-Clause License:

## Symphonia

AAC is decoded by Symphonia (MPL-2.0). `symphonia-core` is built from a modified copy in
`vendor/symphonia-core/` (its FFT tables are built on first use; see its `VENDORED.md`); the
changed file keeps its MPL-2.0 header, and its source is in this repository.

## TLS and cryptography on Linux

On Windows, HTTPS goes through the system's SChannel (native-tls). The Linux builds use rustls,
with the system's certificates (rustls-platform-verifier), or, on a system that has none,
Mozilla's root certificates built in (webpki-root-certs, CDLA Permissive 2.0), and AWS-LC for
the cryptography: the `aws-lc-sys` crate compiles AWS-LC's C code (a fork of BoringSSL, itself
a fork of OpenSSL) into the binary. AWS-LC's notice, which names the code from BoringSSL,
OpenSSL, Fiat Cryptography, s2n-bignum, the Jitter Entropy library and its other sources with
their licences, is the ISC License text listed for aws-lc-sys under
[Licence texts](#licence-texts) below; its MIT License text is Fiat Cryptography's.

## Linux system libraries

The Linux binary also uses libraries of the system it runs on: glibc, libstdc++, FreeType,
Fontconfig and ALSA, and, for the window, the X11 or Wayland client libraries and xkbcommon.
The `.tar.gz` includes none of them. The AppImage includes, for its own copy of the binary,
those of the window's libraries that not every system has, listed below; the rest are the
system's.

## Libraries in the AppImage

The AppImage carries these in `usr/lib/`, as Ubuntu 22.04 builds them, for x86_64 and
aarch64. Their licences are MIT and MIT-style X11 licences; each package's copyright file
follows the table, as Ubuntu ships it.

| Library | Ubuntu 22.04 package | Source |
|---|---|---|
| libxkbcommon, libxkbcommon-x11 | libxkbcommon0, libxkbcommon-x11-0 1.4.0-1 | https://xkbcommon.org/ |
| libxcb-xkb | libxcb-xkb1 1.14-3ubuntu3 | https://xcb.freedesktop.org/ |
| libXcursor | libxcursor1 1:1.2.0-2build4 | https://gitlab.freedesktop.org/xorg/lib/libxcursor |
| libXi | libxi6 2:1.8-1build1 | https://gitlab.freedesktop.org/xorg/lib/libxi |
| libXrender | libxrender1 1:0.9.10-1build4 | https://gitlab.freedesktop.org/xorg/lib/libxrender |
| libXfixes | libxfixes3 1:6.0.0-1 | https://gitlab.freedesktop.org/xorg/lib/libxfixes |
| libXext | libxext6 2:1.3.4-1build1 | https://gitlab.freedesktop.org/xorg/lib/libxext |

```
/// The PUA code points special-cased in the GB18030 encoder.
pub(crate) static GB18030_2022_OVERRIDE_PUA: [u16; 18] = [
    0xE78D, 0xE78E, 0xE78F, 0xE790, 0xE791, 0xE792, 0xE793, 0xE794, 0xE795, 0xE796, 0xE81E, 0xE826,
    0xE82B, 0xE82C, 0xE832, 0xE843, 0xE854, 0xE864,
];

/// The bytes corresponding to the PUA code points special-cased in the GB18030 encoder.
pub(crate) static GB18030_2022_OVERRIDE_BYTES: [[u8; 2]; 18] = [
    [0xA6, 0xD9],
    [0xA6, 0xDA],
    [0xA6, 0xDB],
    [0xA6, 0xDC],
    [0xA6, 0xDD],
    [0xA6, 0xDE],
    [0xA6, 0xDF],
    [0xA6, 0xEC],
    [0xA6, 0xED],
    [0xA6, 0xF3],
    [0xFE, 0x59],
    [0xFE, 0x61],
    [0xFE, 0x66],
    [0xFE, 0x67],
    [0xFE, 0x6D],
    [0xFE, 0x7E],
    [0xFE, 0x90],
    [0xFE, 0xA0],
];
```
